# cstruct-rs2 comparison and benchmark report

## Executive result

Campaign `20260810T202616Z-5f97dcc1` compares candidate `3fef71a52e0637087caa81fd7f77fce52b2c952b` with the frozen source-head environment.
The matrix contains 1491 cells: 1317 exact and 174 non-exact or invalid.
Time-weighted per-plugin command speedup across 1317 equal timed cells is 1.448x.
Only equal source-head/rs2 work contributes to a speed claim. Release 4.7 remains an adjudicator, not a benchmark baseline.

## The three environments, in plain terms

Every command in this report was run against three cstruct stacks, and each one has a different job.

- **Source head** is the reference. It is the current upstream `dissect.cstruct` code at commit `46b850fa`, and it defines what "correct" means here. Every speed number in this report is source-head time divided by rs2 time.
- **rs2** is the candidate being judged, on both correctness and speed.
- **Release 4.7** is the referee. It never contributes a timing. It is only consulted when source head and rs2 disagree, to show which of the two is the odd one out.

That last role is what "adjudicator, not a benchmark baseline" means. When rs2 output differs from source head, the cell is counted as a failure — full stop — but 4.7 tells us _why_ it differs. Sometimes rs2 is wrong. Sometimes source head has changed its own behaviour and rs2 still agrees with the released version, as in cause group `c17d3da81b64fc0f`, where rs2 and 4.7 both recover Windows shortcut records that source head now drops. Either way the cell stays in the non-exact column: agreeing with the referee does not earn a pass, it only explains the disagreement.

Keeping 4.7 out of the timings is a deliberate handicap. 4.7 registers all 1491 cells while source head registers only 1374, so measuring rs2 against 4.7 would quietly add roughly a hundred commands that source head cannot even start — an easy way to publish a bigger number. This report refuses that. A cell earns a speedup only when source head and rs2 ran the same command and produced byte-identical output, which is why the 174 non-exact cells are excluded from timing altogether and the headline figure rests on the 1317 that qualified.

## Compatibility inventory

The union retains 1491 artifact/function cells: source head registers 1374, rs2 registers 1486, and 4.7 registers 1491. Empty functions, aliases, aggregates, missing registrations, required-argument failures and non-record output remain in the matrix.

| Comparison result                              | Cells |
| ---------------------------------------------- | ----: |
| Exact source-head/rs2 output                   |  1317 |
| Different, invalid, or source-nondeterministic |   174 |

## Incompatibilities and adjudication

Every non-exact cell has source/self and 4.7 captures. The following groups are the deduplicated causes; the complete row-to-group mapping is in the machine-readable export.

| Cause ID         | Rows | Component                                           | Ownership                                                        |
| ---------------- | ---: | --------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 0449c649686b6997 |   18 | dissect.target Windows registry applications plugin | shared downstream invocation failure; not an rs2 output mismatch |

The plugin passes a registry value decoded as an integer to datetime.strptime(), which requires a string. All three environments exit at applications.py:65.

Evidence paths: `dissect.target/plugins/os/windows/regf/applications.py:65`.

| 30a99767291149a4 | 55 | source-head target-query registration | source-head registration difference; no rs2 speed claim |

The source-head CLI rejects the listed functions as invalid plugins, while the rs2 and 4.7 registrations accept them. The source-head stack cannot provide an equal-work baseline.

Evidence paths: `dissect.target/tools/query.py argument validation`, `inventory registration evidence`.

| 35dc87f7b83e9dde | 9 | source-head target-query registration | source-head registration difference; no rs2 speed claim |

The source-head CLI rejects these functions as invalid plugins; the registered rs2 and 4.7 controls complete. This is retained as a source-head registration difference.

Evidence paths: `dissect.target/tools/query.py argument validation`, `inventory registration evidence`.

| 4dd925635d729399 | 1 | journal dynamic records | rs2 incompatibility, with a separate source-head registration difference |

Source head does not register journal. rs2 reaches the plugin but rejects a journal EntryArrayObject because its stream probe exceeds MAX_PROBE; 4.7 completes the same command.

Evidence paths: `crates/cstruct-rs2-py/src/types/decoding.rs:grow_or_fail`, `dissect.target/plugins/os/unix/log/journal.py:361`.

| 52ce782e8acc6245 | 1 | ad.secretsdump output contract | source-head registration and shared output-contract failure; not an rs2 output mismatch |

Source head rejects the function as unregistered. rs2 and 4.7 both exit successfully but emit non-record lines under -j, which the strict JSON record capture correctly rejects.

Evidence paths: `dissect.target/tools/query.py argument validation`, `measurement capture JSON-line classifier`.

| 5b1406bdc09b351f | 2 | source-head record ordering | source-head nondeterminism; not an rs2 output mismatch |

The source/self control has the same record multiset but a different sequence. The order-only result is proved nondeterministic by the control and is excluded from timing.

Evidence paths: `source/self capture sequence hashes`, `measurement capture ordered digest comparison`.

| 69a6e8cd7652d86a | 2 | source-head event-log registration | source-head integration failure; no rs2 speed claim |

The Defender plugin calls target.evtx(), but source head cannot resolve that function. rs2 and 4.7 complete; source/self reproduces the same PluginNotFoundError.

Evidence paths: `dissect.target/plugins/os/windows/defender.py`, `dissect.target/target.py:get_function`.

| 809cac07373b7792 | 46 | source-head target-query registration | source-head registration difference; no rs2 speed claim |

The source-head CLI rejects the exported functions as invalid plugins while rs2 and 4.7 execute them. The registration union intentionally retains these rows.

Evidence paths: `dissect.target/tools/query.py argument validation`, `inventory registration evidence`.

| c17d3da81b64fc0f | 8 | Windows shortcut parser | source-head behavioral change; rs2 matches the 4.7 adjudicator, but remains non-exact against the required source-head baseline |

cstruct source head 46b850f moves internal structure attributes to dunder names. dissect.shellitem.lnk reads generated SHELL_LINK_HEADER fields while dissect.target's lnk plugin catches the resulting parse failures, so source head drops records. rs2 and 4.7 agree on the additional records.

Evidence paths: `dissect.cstruct commit 46b850fa9cd023a9de583149caa5e9ba31254cf1`, `dissect.shellitem/lnk/lnk.py:_parse_header`, `dissect.target/plugins/os/windows/lnk.py:lnk`.

| c91b3420921218bb | 1 | Docker local-log custom type | rs2 incompatibility, with a separate source-head custom-type ABI failure |

Source head passes the new endian keyword to the downstream ProtobufVarint custom type and fails. rs2 independently fails to resolve the dynamic s_len length in the local-log record; 4.7 completes.

Evidence paths: `dissect.cstruct/types/base.py:MetaType.__call__`, `dissect.target/plugins/apps/container/docker.py:c_local.entry`, `crates/cstruct-rs2-core/src/decode/layout.rs`.

| fbdf89d56763c3f3 | 6 | source-head event-log registration | source-head integration failure; no rs2 speed claim |

The Defender plugin reproduces the source-head target.evtx() lookup failure while rs2 and 4.7 complete.

Evidence paths: `dissect.target/plugins/os/windows/defender.py`, `dissect.target/target.py:get_function`.

| fc493e9ca4e44d59 | 25 | required target-query arguments | shared invocation failure; not an rs2 output mismatch |

The selected functions require an output or environment-path argument, and the reviewed argument manifest supplies none. All controls reject the same incomplete command rather than doing comparable work.

Evidence paths: `dissect.target/tools/query.py CLI argument parser`, `comparison argument manifest`.

## Timing method and raw evidence

Each valid workload was run exactly twice per implementation in the fixed source-head, rs2, rs2, source-head order. The plugin and artifact matrices print all four elapsed samples. Each raw timing entry also retains exit status, peak RSS, user and system CPU time, load averages, available memory, execution order and the exact command. Pair ratios and the one-sided 95% interval are retained in `comparison-data.json`; with two pairs they are variability evidence, not a confidence claim.
